- **workflow-resume**: Find recent dynamic workflow runs that lost agents to API errors, and resume or finish them correctly. A workflow agent that hits a rate limit, usage limit, dropped connection or 5xx returns null with zero retries; parallel()/pipeline() map that to null too, the generated script's closing .filter(Boolean) drops it, and the run reports `completed` with the loss unreported. This skill separates the two shapes that need opposite handling: a finished run with dead agents, and a run that is still live (which must not be relaunched, and whose worktrees and pid-held locks must not be touched). Bundl… ([DiologIR/diolog-plugins](https://github.com/DiologIR/diolog-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/workflow-resume) · 1 skill)
